Paint defense was the top community request and 'biggest gameplay improvement' in external playtests (Wang). | Interior Defense / Strength ratings; 2K24 badges Immovable Enforcer (defense) vs Bulldozer/Physical Handles/Blow-By (offense); 94 Feet reduces body-up stamina hits when pressing full court. | Not stated. | https://blog.playstation.com/2023/08/21/how-new-nba-2k24-gameplay-features-improve-newcomer-experience-out-sep-8-on-ps5-and-ps4/ |
| On-the-fly Takeover selection, Double Takeover, Mamba Mentality | 2K24 (New Gen) | badges_takeover | Takeover is no longer chosen in the builder. When the meter fills, a menu lets you pick Finishing, Shooting, Playmaking, Defense/Rebounding, or Physicals; activating boosts all ratings in that category (stacks on existing ratings). Delaying activation to fill a second meter enables Double Takeover (two categories). Unlocking Mamba Mentality makes Double Takeover boost ALL categories. | 5 categories; 2 meters; meters display percentages. Takeover Perks retained (OP ones toned down): Decelerator, Minimizer, Suppressor, Sponge, Extender, Juice, Stay Warm, Accelerator (unlock conditions listed at nba2kw). | Not stated for 2K25. | https://nba.2k.com/2k24/courtside-report/gameplay/ |
| 2K Smart Play (return) and Favorite Plays (16 slots) | 2K24 | ai_tendencies | Simplified playcalling. Smart Play: after any made/missed shot an overlay shows the play type that will be called; tap L1/LB before crossing halfcourt to opt in and the action auto-targets the player with the most relevant play type. Favorite Plays: D-pad Left opens up to 16 preferred actions (full plays, isos, give-and-gos). | L1/LB tap before halfcourt; D-pad Left; 16 slots (starter/bench barrier removed so all 16 are shared). 5 Double Action mini-plays: Give Flare (handoff -> flare screen), Give Fist (handoff -> ball screen), Fist Flare (ball screen -> flare screen), Fist Screen (ball screen -> off-ball screen), Screen Fist (off-ball screen -> ball screen). | Not stated. | https://nba.2k.com/2k24/courtside-report/gameplay/ |
| Comp Freelance and Motion Freelance (Da Czar) | 2K24 | ai_tendencies | Comp freelance: for competitive users who want to control all teammate movement; AI spaces 5-out, no automatic pass or cut actions, everything triggered via the 16 favorite plays; teammate cut-to-basket setting Auto/Manual (freelance only, not set plays). Motion freelance: no set form (morphs 3-out/4-out/5-out), auto-runs actions for the best players on the floor, updatable via roster updates. | Cut setting: Auto (AI cuts on opportunity) / Manual (only on user command). | Not stated. | https://nba.2k.com/2k24/courtside-report/gameplay/ |
| Lineups & substitution engine (scorer-aware rotations) | 2K24 | ai_tendencies | Replaces AI tendency to sub all five at once (which caused AI scoring droughts). Minutes are distributed to keep a scorer on the floor; supports 6th-man lineups and closing lineups distinct from starters; built on the ACE game-plan engine so offensive focus changes with the lineup. | Data for 1-scorer, 2-scorer, and 3-scorer lineups. | Not stated. | https://nba.2k.com/2k24/courtside-report/gameplay/ |
| Height-tiered badges (S/A/B/C) with usage-based progression and regression | 2K24 (New Gen) | badges_takeover | No badge points or equip choices: all badges a build qualifies for are active, and each badge's level trends toward the level matching actual in-game usage/success. Unused badges regress; floor is Bronze; regaining a lost level is faster than first earning it; badges that cannot fire in a mode (e.g., Relay Passer in 2v2) are protected. Tier is set by player height; each badge has attribute requirements per level. Higher tiers are harder to gain and quicker to lose. Practice modes (Team Practice Facility, Gatorade Training Facility, Chris Brickley's Gym) only ever progress badges. | 77 skill badges total (24 new; removed: Limitless Takeoff, Bully, Clamp Breaker, Menace, Mismatch Expert, Quick First Step, Vice Grip, Amped, Clutch Shooter, Floor General, Volume Shooter). Example thresholds (nba2kw tables): 94 Feet Bronze/Silver/Gold/HOF = 76/81/88/93 Perimeter D + 87/90/95/99 Stamina, max height 6'8"; Posterizer Gold = 93 Driving Dunk + 80 Vertical; Green Machine Gold = 88 Mid + 89 3PT; Open Looks Bronze = 54 Mid/58 3PT. NLSC example: 6'4" SG has Clamps in S tier, HOF needs 99 Perimeter D + 60 Acceleration. Rebirth: +5% (1.0) / +10% (2.0) badge progression boost. | 2K25 removed regression entirely (Floor Setters dropped as unnecessary), cut to 40 badges in 2 height-based tiers, added Legend level above HOF, and merged many badges (e.g., Blinders into Deadeye). | https://web.archive.org/web/2023id_/https://nba.2k.com/2k24/courtside-report/badges/ |
| Badge Perks, Performance Multipliers, Floor Setters | 2K24 | badges_takeover | Meta-progression modifiers. Badge Perks apply to one badge each (4 slots per perk, one per tier; one perk per badge): Overdrive (+progression in games), Immunity (slower regression), Drill Savant (+progression in Team Practice), Scholar (big boost in PvP with high Teammate Grade, zero otherwise; seasonal), Winner's Circle (boost on PvP wins, zero on losses; seasonal), High-Risk (more progression with usage, faster regression without; seasonal). Performance Multipliers apply to all equipped badges per mode (NBA, City, REC, Pro-Am): Grade A Student, Winner Takes All, Up for the Challenge (12 total). Floor Setters: permanent per-badge minimum level, auto-progresses badge to that level if attribute requirements met. | 18 Floor Setter slots (9 Silver, 9 Gold) via free Season Pass XP. Perk unlock examples (nba2kw): Overdrive C-tier via Josh Giddey after multiple Silver badges; B-tier after 5 NBA / 10 Pro-Am-REC / 20 City games with perk equipped; A-tier 7/12/30; S-tier 12/20/40. Scholar tiers at Season Level 9/18/29/38. | 2K25 kept Perks with new set (Max +1, Participation, Synergy, Overdrive returning), kept Performance Multipliers, added Badge Elevators, removed Floor Setters. | https://web.archive.org/web/2023id_/https://nba.2k.com/2k24/courtside-report/badges/ |
| MyPLAYER NBA templates and anti-meta builder changes | 2K24 (New Gen) | other | Builder offers customizable NBA player templates; accepting a template as-is starts career with that player's signature animations equipped even at 60 OVR. Attribute caps for size combinations overhauled, max heights/weights per position updated, archetype names made more descriptive, Takeover removed from builder. | Height determines badge tier placement; per-position height/weight caps (values in Beluba tweets, not captured here). | 2K25 builder continued templates and added further changes (not covered here). | https://web.archive.org/web/2023id_/https://nba.2k.com/2k24/courtside-report/badges/ |
| Pro Stick Rhythm Shooting (NOT a 2K24 mechanic - correction to task landmark) | 2K25 | shooting | The task listed 'Pro Stick rhythm shooting' as a 2K24 landmark. The 2K25 Gameplay Courtside Report states: 'While all shooting control methods from NBA 2K24 are returning, NBA 2K25 introduces a brand new way to shoot called Pro Stick Rhythm Shooting. Instead of simply pressing and releasing the shot button or stick, you first pull the shot stick DOWN to start the shot.' Beluba (2K26): rhythm shooting is about matching the shot's tempo; there is still an ideal timing point for pushing the Pro Stick, better timing gives more tempo wiggle room; speed of stick throw affects animation. | RS down to start, RS up matched to tempo (2K25+). 2K24's Pro Stick shooting was the legacy hold-and-release stick shot. | Introduced 2K25, retained 2K26. | https://nba.2k.com/2k25/courtside-report/gameplay |

## NBA 2K25 (Sep 2024) – NBA 2K26 (Sep 2025) – NBA 2K27 (Sep 2026), Gen 9 / ProPLAY line

Sources read: 23. Gaps: 1) nba.2k.com does not resolve from this session's WebFetch; all official pages were read through the Parallel fetch tool (2K25 and 2K26 gameplay reports and 2K27 gameplay/roadmap/builder pages read in full; 2K25 and 2K26 MyPLAYER builder pages and 2K26 game guide read as excerpts only). 2) The 2K27 'Courtside Report' is primarily a YouTube video (Tyrese Maxey + Mike Wang); the written counterpart is nba.2k.com/2k27/features/gameplay/, which was read. Video transcripts were not read. 3) Mike Wang's X/Discord/Reddit quotes were read via the nba2klab and nba2kw compilations (secondary transcriptions), not the original posts, except two X posts seen as search excerpts (Beluba 2093003180561018965; The2KCentral 1964413744156664293). Treat exact wording as compilation-sourced. 4) No frame counts were published by devs for any window; the only ms figures (rhythm tempo 35-60 ms, Haliburton push timing ~540-570 ms) are nba2klab community measurements, UNCONFIRMED. 5) Percentages confirmed by Wang: Very Quick -10% / Quick -5% release-speed window penalty, 10% meter-off boost (2K27), game speed +10% (2K26), 2K27 per-mode speed 5-7% slower offline / +2% 3v3; momentum-cross BH tiers 85+/60-84/<60; J. Williams Euro 75+ Driving Layup & <6'5. 6) Patch-note pages were scanned by keyword, not read line by line; 2K25 patch dates were not extracted; 2K26 patch dates taken from headers (v2.0 Oct 14 2025, v2.1 Oct 17 2025). 7) No gameplay Courtside Report by Da Czar or Erick Boenisch exists for this era (Boenisch authored City reports); all three gameplay reports are signed/presented by Mike Wang. 8) Seasonal Courtside Reports (S1-S8 each year) and the 2K26 Presentation report were not read; they contain mostly rewards, not mechanics. 9) 'Shot Timing Ability' slider range, Takeover ability durations, Synergy reaction activation counts, and number of 'defender types' in 2K27 were not quantified by devs. 10) Patch note regression: 2K26 'ghost contests' fixed in v2.0 and again flagged/fixed in 2K27 v2.0, indicating the contest-position-at-release rule was re-tuned twice.

| Mechanic | Introduced | System | What it does | Inputs / tunables | Later | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ProPLAY animation expansion (2K25) | 2K25 (2024) | other | Motion-capture animations replaced by animations translated directly from NBA broadcast footage. 9,000 new ProPLAY animations in 2K25, total library ~14,600. Devs' breakdown of the new 9,000: 1,500 dribble sequences, 1,100 signature shots (most 2023-24 rostered players), 1,300 motion sequences incl. 150 signature motion styles, 800 rebounds, 1,000 passes, 434 dunks, 550 blocks, 1,110 layups. | Counts above (Courtside Report + Mike Wang Discord via nba2klab). 2K27 adds 'over 7,000 new animations' (2K newsroom). | Continued every year; 2K26 adds ML Motion Engine, 2K27 7,000+ more. | https://nba.2k.com/2k25/courtside-report/gameplay/ |
| ProPLAY Dribble Engine | 2K25 (2024) | dribbling | Dribble movement no longer driven by hand-authored pre-scripted transitions from a mocap library; generated dynamically from NBA footage as 'one holistic piece of motion'. Devs call it the biggest engine change in 15 years. Dribble styles went from 25 to 37; each style has 20-40 unique ProPLAY sequences (vs 4-5 before); engine driven by at least 6x the motion data. All advanced combos (e.g. shammgod) moved to right stick instead of RS->LS branching. | Dribble launch speed determined solely by new 'Speed With Ball' attribute (Acceleration removed); defensive lateral movement/off-ball launches use new 'Agility' attribute. 37 dribble styles. Player-to-court ratio adjusted to give more room. | 2K26: branching/chaining made more interruptible and faster; 2K27: devs say they went 'back to a one-to-one system' with new blend/transition tech. | https://nba.2k.com/2k25/courtside-report/gameplay/ |
| Signature Go-To Shots | 2K25 (2024) | shooting | Extension of 2K24 Dribble Breakdowns: holding Pro Stick UP triggers a player-specific dribble-into-shot combo (e.g. Curry/Haliburton dribble-stepback jumper) captured via ProPLAY. Release at top of shot animation. | Hold RS UP while standing with ball (no Turbo). Patch note: player breaks out of Go-To Shot if moved too far from marker in drills. | 2K26 adds Signature Go-To Post Shots (RS UP in post). Still present in 2K27 (Luka stepback 3 cited by Wang). | https://nba.2k.com/2k25/courtside-report/gameplay/ |
| Shot Canceling | 2K25 (2024) | shooting | Any advanced shot (stepback, Go-To, etc.) can be cancelled at any time into another move (hesi, shot fake, drive). | No numbers given. 2K26 dev note: Post Go-To shots that include a dribble are cancelable the same way; pump fake chains to step-throughs. | Retained. | https://nba.2k.com/2k25/courtside-report/gameplay/ |
| Shot Timing Profiles (Low / Normal / High Risk-Reward) | 2K25 (2024) | shooting | Player-selectable risk model. Low: forgiving timing, outcome emphasizes ratings/openness; mistimed shots can go in but green window is smaller and timing bonus small. High: larger green window with bigger bonus but strictly green-or-miss. Applies to Rhythm Shooting too. Separate Layup Timing Profiles: Real Player %, Low, Normal, High. | Default 'Difficulty Based' maps Rookie-Pro -> Low, All-Star/Superstar -> Normal, Hall of Fame -> High. Wang: 2K24 sat 'between normal and high'. | REMOVED in 2K26: 'Shot and Layup Timing Risk-Reward settings have been removed' (Wang, Discord). Replaced by universal Green-or-Miss with difficulty-scaled windows. | https://www.nba2klab.com/nba-2k25-mike-wang-faqs |
| Custom Visual Cues (any point in shot) | 2K25 (2024) | shooting | 2K24 offered four visual cue points (Jump, Set Point, Push, Release). 2K25 Jump Shot Creator lets the cue be set to any point in the custom shot after choosing base, release and release speed. | Visual cue does NOT affect Rhythm Shooting grading (always Set Point->Release match). 2K27: shot cues confirmed still present. | Retained through 2K27. | https://nba.2k.com/2k25/courtside-report/gameplay/ |
| Pro Stick Rhythm Shooting (v1) | 2K25 (2024) | shooting | Alternate shot input: pull RS DOWN to start the shot, then at the player's Set Point push RS UP matching the speed of the real player's push motion. Two graded components: Push Timing (moment you begin moving the stick, ideally at Set Point) and Tempo (speed of stick travel to the opposite side, ideally finishing exactly as ball leaves fingertips). Always on and optional: system treats any re-deflection of the Pro Stick to the opposite side as a rhythm shot; releasing stick to center is a normal stick shot. | DOWN-hold then UP flick on RS. 'Higher upside' than button; cannot be compared 1:1. Feels slower than Skill Dunk double-throws, esp. deliberate shooters (Jokic, Kyle Anderson). | 2K26: reworked so only Tempo must be greened (timing just widens tempo window) and stick input drives upper-body animation. 2K27: reverted to requiring greened Timing; tempo only modifies window size ('closer to 25 than 26'). 2K26 v2.0 patch (Oct 14 2025) tightened windows. | https://nba.2k.com/2k25/courtside-report/gameplay/ |
| Timeline shot meters (Arrow / Ring / Dial) | 2K25 (2024) | shooting | Meters no longer freeze on release; all three animate from start to finish in sync with the ideal release so the ideal release is 'the exact frame the meter disappears'. Framed as a shot-animation timeline, making meters latency-independent online for shots, layups and FTs. | Size/color/placement customizable in Customize HUD. Small bonus for meter off (2K25). 2K26: these 2K25 dial/arrow/ring options are the only meters that do NOT show the green window. | 2K26 reintroduced green-window-showing meters (2K24 style) as default; 2K27 meter gains live tick mark synced to RS. | https://www.nba2klab.com/nba-2k25-mike-wang-faqs |
| Simple vs All Shots feedback | 2K25 (2024) | shooting | 'Simple' shot feedback (default On) shows timing result immediately on release; 'All Shots' adds defensive coverage and is delayed as in 2K24. | Customize HUD setting. 2K26: both show at same time; celebrations and feedback become dynamic on shot quality (ratings, coverage, timing/tempo) -> insta-green on excellent open shots. Coverage percentages shown again in 2K26. | 2K27: reworked Shot Feedback overlays break down Timing early/late and Tempo fast/slow; 'slightlys' color coded white/yellow/red. | https://www.nba2klab.com/nba-2k25-mike-wang-faqs |
| Rating-weighted shooting model (2K25) | 2K25 (2024) | shooting | Shot ratings play a 'much larger role'; good timing/rhythm can compensate 'to some degree' but most players need higher ratings to shoot at 2K24 clips. | Patch: standing floaters use Close Shot; driving floaters use weighted average of the higher of Driving Layup and Close Shot. Free throws re-tuned so FT rating plays bigger role. | 2K26 moved to pure green window; 2K27 Wang: 'everything that affects shooting (boosts, ratings, badges, defensive impact, hot zones, stamina, etc.) get fed into... the size of the green window'. | https://www.nba2klab.com/nba-2k25-mike-wang-faqs |
| Optional Layup Timing + Layup Timing Profiles | 2K25 (2024) | shooting | Layup timing disabled by default in 2K25, recommended for slashers; separate Layup Timing Profile options RP%/Low/Normal/High. | Hold RS / shot button through layup, release in window. | 2K26: Layup Timing permanently enabled, layups green-or-miss, meter-off gives slightly larger window. 2K27: layups incl. floaters green-or-miss, 10% window boost with meter off; late contests weaker than 2K26 and meter starts 'somewhat small' so window is more stable. | https://www.nba2klab.com/nba-2k25-mike-wang-faqs |
| Dynamic Dunk Meter (Skill Dunks) | 2K25 (2024) | shooting | Dunk meter window no longer fixed at dunk start; adjusts throughout the sequence to reflect defensive impact. Skill dunks always timed, other dunks optional. 500+ new ProPLAY dunks; controllable rim hangs on 1-handed dunks by keeping RT/R2 held. Jab steps under basket replaced by pump fakes. | 2K26: meter dunk = Turbo + RS DOWN; much bigger green window on open dunks; standing skill dunks given larger windows to compensate for speed. | 2K27: ALL dunk attempts must be timed (every RS direction and the button); fully dynamic window that can expand or shrink through the finish; dunk timing can be disabled offline; a legacy 'ball slips on collision' feature flagged for removal. v2.0 patch reduced missed dunks with timing off and fixed misses on correct timing. | https://www.nba2klab.com/nba-2k25-mike-wang-faqs |
| Defensive Movement System / Defensive Cutoffs (v1) | 2K25 (2024) | defense | Defender can use the right stick to slide laterally and cut off a drive, mirroring the offense's RS direction changes. A successful anticipated cutoff stonewalls the ball handler or causes a fumble. | With Intense-D (LT/L2) held, RS = cutoff moves; release LT for RS steals. Cutoffs cost no adrenaline (2K25). User-controlled defenders get slight contest-score boost vs AI teammates; AI teammates have rating-based perception delays. | 2K27: expanded to two cutoffs (flick RS = one-step; R2/RT + RS flick = larger lunge), powered by Speed and Agility; successful cutoff body-up drains ball handler Adrenaline and can force fumble/pickup. RS steals REMOVED in 2K27 in favor of cutoffs. | https://nba.2k.com/2k25/courtside-report/gameplay/ |
| Dynamic-weighted Contest System | 2K25 (2024) | defense | Contest evaluation switched from fixed to dynamic weighting between defensive impact at shot start vs at release. Perimeter/interior defense ratings and height differential made much stronger factors. | Patch: 'hand down' situations no longer register as Tight; open shots slightly buffed. 2K26: rebalanced hands-up / no-jump / jumping contests; defender who jumps early and is landing at release has impact 'drop considerably' (also in paint). 2K27 Wang: height is the stronger factor vs Perimeter D; Block attribute only helps target the ball. | 2K26 v2.0 and 2K27 v2.0 patches both addressed 'ghost contests'; 2K27 contests now require defender squarely in front at release. | https://nba.2k.com/2k25/courtside-report/gameplay/ |

Player-to-court ratio adjusted. | 2K26 adds MyPLAYER Freelance Engine (AI considers user's off-ball position) and Drive-and-Kick module; 2K27 adds spacing-lane logic for early offense. | https://nba.2k.com/2k25/courtside-report/gameplay/ |

| AI difficulty beyond shooting % + scaled on-ball delays | 2K25 (2024) | ai_tendencies | Difficulty differentiation extended past shooting percentages: lower difficulties have less aggressive O/D and fewer strategy adjustments; on-ball defensive reaction delays scaled per difficulty; increased body-up win resolutions for easier blow-bys on low difficulty. | Per-difficulty delay scaling (no numbers). | 2K26 adds 'ten new variables' (help rotation awareness, help commit speed, pass-lane recognition speed, etc.) and a separate 'Shot Timing Ability' CPU slider. | https://nba.2k.com/2k25/courtside-report/gameplay/ |

| Enhanced Rhythm Shooting (tempo-driven, animation-reactive) | 2K26 (2025) | shooting | RS DOWN-UP flick now manipulates the shooter's upper body: early/fast flick = rushed quick release, late/slow flick = delayed push and lazier follow-through. Make condition changed: you do NOT have to green the Timing, only the Tempo; excellent timing just widens the tempo window. Hanging at set point longer demands more precise tempo; a ceiling on set-point hold auto-starts the motion. | Wang: 'Timing is WHEN you start pushing RS down to up; Tempo is HOW FAST you move it.' Intentionally rushing shrinks green tempo window. Button vs rhythm designed with 'neither having an advantage'. Community-measured (nba2klab, UNCONFIRMED by devs): tempo 35-60 ms, Haliburton push timing ~540-570 ms. | 2K26 v2.0 patch (Oct 14 2025): input reliability improved and Tempo+Timing windows slightly tightened. 2K27: reverted to requiring greened Timing; tempo only scales window. | https://nba.2k.com/2k26/courtside-report/gameplay/ |

Rookie ('outside even a pixel... miss'); windows tighter than 2K26 overall but bigger on Rookie/Semi-Pro; non-green makes only via deflection or physics failure. | https://www.nba2klab.com/nba-2k26-mike-wang-faqs |
